{"parentUuid":"c6f31c38-d827-47f7-bb99-e538a9c51afa","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"57b56c9a-6033-4ec3-9d78-69d7d379e66e","timestamp":"2026-05-11T14:18:27.682Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"57b56c9a-6033-4ec3-9d78-69d7d379e66e","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- autonomous: /autonomous — Toggle Autonomous Mode (on/off/status)\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":51,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"870355a8-eebb-4767-8792-a5557a77d98c","timestamp":"2026-05-11T14:18:27.682Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"0a35d538-0ac2-4359-8124-fc64e9f2f75b","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"857ad5a7-8f3f-4534-a6f9-702bc1f4aa10","timestamp":"2026-05-11T14:18:47.284Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"814d9ff2-a036-4d84-a3db-59f552abfb62","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# human-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents reading anything inside `human-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`human-workspace/` is the **human-authored authority** layer. Everything here represents **human decisions, scope, intent, and verification**. Agents READ this directory as authoritative; agents do NOT WRITE here except in one designated channel (`q-and-a/pending/` — where agents post questions for human to answer).\n\nThis separation exists because of a failure mode observed in the orch sister project (orch `decisions/040`, post-mortem CF-DOGFOOD-2): when human and agent share a single workspace without contract, agent silently overrides human-CRITICAL items via technical-defer logic. The user explicitly raised this concern in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_02_init.txt` §1.1.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Who writes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `user_prompt/` | Human's mid-flight scope/idea drops. Naming: `YYYYMMDD_NN_<slug>.txt`. Files are immutable post-creation (audit trail). | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `decisions/` | Human's strategic decisions (e.g., scope-lock, charter-revision-trigger, peer-share authorization). Format: `D-H-<NNN>-<slug>.md`. | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `q-and-a/pending/` | **The ONE channel where agent writes inside human-workspace**. Agent drops Q&A bundles per Grill Maximization doctrine; human responds inline or moves to `answered/`. | **Both** (agent writes new bundles; human writes answers). |\n| `q-and-a/answered/` | Q&A bundles human has answered (with answers inline). Agent reads answers and acts. Archive forever; provenance trail. | **Human moves** (drag from `pending/`); agent reads. |\n| `q-and-a/stale/` | Pending bundles that exceeded their `expected_answer_by` (default 24h, urgent 4h). Auto-moved by SessionStart hook (Track 5). | **Hook moves**; both read. |\n| `notifications/` | Agent push to human — summaries, alerts, milestones. Format: `N-<TS>-<level>-<slug>.md`. Levels: INFO / SUMMARY / ALERT. | **Agent writes**; human reads. |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Agent NEVER writes to `user_prompt/` or `decisions/`.** Permission deny. If something feels like a human decision but the human hasn't written it, dispatch a Q&A bundle into `q-and-a/pending/` instead.\n\n2. **`user_prompt/` files are immutable post-creation.** Agent never deletes, renames, or edits these files. They are the audit trail of human interventions.\n\n3. **Q&A bundle format** (per Track 4 spec): YAML frontmatter (`id`, `topic`, `opened_at`, `expected_answer_by`, `priority`, `related_decisions`, `status`) + question body + answer-fill section. Each bundle = up to 25 questions per Grill Maximization doctrine; split if more.\n\n4. **Agent does not move files between `q-and-a/{pending,answered,stale}/`** — that's human's role (or hook's, in case of stale). Agent only WRITES new pending bundles and READS answers.\n\n5. **Notifications are read-only for human.** Human shouldn't edit `notifications/*` files; if disagreement with notification content, drop a `user_prompt/` instead. Notifications are the agent's account of events.\n\n6. **Provenance pointers**: every `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-<NNN>` should reference its source `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>` or `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-<NNN>` if the agent's decision derives from human input.\n\n7. **No multi-tenancy in this directory.** Per identity-scope.md (Track 7), stockforge is single-tenant Phase 0-5; if peer share happens it's via git-fork, each peer has their own `human-workspace/` per fork.\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nWhen loading session context (per `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` Track 7):\n\n1. **All `user_prompt/*.txt`** — every prompt, not just most recent. Per orch lesson (decision-040 USER-CRITICAL tier): never silently defer human-CRITICAL items.\n2. **All `decisions/*.md`** — human's strategic decisions are above charter principles in priority; charter is below human (charter was authored by human in the first place).\n3. **Latest `q-and-a/answered/*`** — recent answers update agent's understanding.\n4. **Open `q-and-a/pending/*`** — flag if waiting on human answers.\n5. **Recent `notifications/*`** (last 7 days) — for situational awareness.\n\n---\n\n## Agent Self-Check\n\nBefore writing to `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`, ask:\n- Is this question genuinely something the human must decide? (Charter principle / Charter-affecting / SCOPE-affecting / above ARCH-tier confidence threshold)\n- Have I already grilled this in a recent bundle? (Don't ask twice; don't pollute pending/)\n- Am I bundling per Grill Maximization (target 15-20 questions, max 25)? Or splintering across many small bundles?\n\nIf the answer to any is \"I'm not sure\" — DON'T write the bundle. Either answer it yourself with charter-principle reasoning + log decision, or wait until ambiguity clears.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"21d18318-a586-4d87-98f5-cea0902b90ae","timestamp":"2026-05-11T14:18:51.998Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"d7810ab8-4c99-490a-9373-9a4c8b1d9308","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9d95ee46-5ae7-4d8e-b33d-12647cbb0377","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MgeyxqgvxbEYvFuTn4fFiu","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# PROJECT CHARTER\n2\t## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n3\t\n4\t> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n5\t> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n6\t> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## Vision\n11\t\n12\tAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n13\t\n14\tPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n15\t\n16\tPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n17\t\n18\tNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## The Core Insight (the why)\n23\t\n24\t**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n25\t\n26\t**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n27\t- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n28\t- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n29\t- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n30\t- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n31\t- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n32\t- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n33\t\n34\tIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n35\t\n36\tThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n37\t\n38\t---\n39\t\n40\t## The Craft Philosophy\n41\t\n42\tTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n43\t\n44\t- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n45\t- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n46\t- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n47\t- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n48\t- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n49\t\n50\tReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n51\t\n52\t---\n53\t\n54\t## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n55\t\n56\t1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n57\t\n58\t2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n59\t\n60\t3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent."}]},"uuid":"c259bc16-b020-4056-8d82-4ce7d2fb2dbe","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:49:03.781Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\PROJECT_CHARTER.md","content":"# PROJECT CHARTER\n## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n\n> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n\n---\n\n## Vision\n\nAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n\nPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n\nPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n\nNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n\n---\n\n## The Core Insight (the why)\n\n**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n\n**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n\nIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n\nThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n\n---\n\n## The Craft Philosophy\n\nTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n\n- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n\nReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n\n---\n\n## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n\n1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n\n2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n\n3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent.","numLines":60,"startLine":1,"totalLines":257}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d7810ab8-4c99-490a-9373-9a4c8b1d9308","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"537a79fc-a379-4f48-8875-e8a20e826e43","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9d95ee46-5ae7-4d8e-b33d-12647cbb0377","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012USGgtZPmtJ1GncjcyBxaA","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# charter-coherence-spot.sh — greps for charter-incoherent language in recent agent outputs.\n3\t# Detects: \"buy/sell/recommend\" without \"thesis exploration\" framing (violates I-S35);\n4\t# LLM-output numerics without code-source citation; financial-advice phrasing.\n5\t# Adapted from orch scripts/verify/charter_coherence_spot_check.* (A-6 in borrow-list).\n6\t# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B deliverable.\n7\tset -uo pipefail\n8\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n9\t\n10\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n11\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n12\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.charter-coherence-violations.log\"\n13\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n14\t\n15\t# Files to audit: recent thesis-log, agent outputs, anything claiming to be advice.\n16\tTARGETS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n17\t                \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\" \\\n18\t                \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" \\\n19\t            -type f -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -30)\"\n20\t[ -z \"$TARGETS\" ] && exit 0\n21\t\n22\tVIOLATIONS=0\n23\t\n24\twhile IFS= read -r f; do\n25\t  [ -z \"$f\" ] || [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n26\t  # I-S35: charter framing — \"buy\" / \"sell\" / \"recommend\" / \"should invest\" must coexist with\n27\t  # \"thesis exploration\" / \"consideration\" / \"research aid\" framing.\n28\t  if grep -qiE '\\b(buy this|sell this|should buy|should sell|recommend.{0,20}(stock|share|invest)|definitely (buy|sell)|guaranteed return)\\b' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n29\t    if ! grep -qiE '(thesis exploration|research aid|consideration|not financial advice|not advice)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n30\t      VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n31\t      printf '[%s] I-S35-VIOLATION (advice-without-aid-framing) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n32\t    fi\n33\t  fi\n34\t  # I-S1: bare percentages outside code blocks claiming to be analysis output.\n35\t  if grep -qiE '(my analysis|i (calculated|computed|estimate|think))[^.]*[0-9]+%' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n36\t    VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n37\t    printf '[%s] I-S1-VIOLATION (LLM-self-claim-of-computation) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n38\t  fi\n39\t  # Insider information signal: \"insider\" / \"leaked\" / \"private channel\" — flag for review.\n40\t  if grep -qiE '\\b(insider info|leaked from|private channel|paid leak|confidential source)\\b' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n41\t    VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n42\t    printf '[%s] CHARTER-VIOLATION (insider-info-signal) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n43\t  fi\n44\tdone <<< \"$TARGETS\"\n45\t\n46\tif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n47\t  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] charter-coherence-spot: $VIOLATIONS violation(s) — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n48\t  printf '[charter-coherence] WARN: %d charter-incoherent pattern(s) detected. See %s\\n' \"$VIOLATIONS\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n49\tfi\n50\t\n51\texit 0\n52\t"}]},"uuid":"8b8c8b26-78d5-4a04-86a3-dc279e406d24","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:49:04.372Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\charter-coherence-spot.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# charter-coherence-spot.sh — greps for charter-incoherent language in recent agent outputs.\n# Detects: \"buy/sell/recommend\" without \"thesis exploration\" framing (violates I-S35);\n# LLM-output numerics without code-source citation; financial-advice phrasing.\n# Adapted from orch scripts/verify/charter_coherence_spot_check.* (A-6 in borrow-list).\n# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B deliverable.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.charter-coherence-violations.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\n# Files to audit: recent thesis-log, agent outputs, anything claiming to be advice.\nTARGETS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n                \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\" \\\n                \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" \\\n            -type f -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -30)\"\n[ -z \"$TARGETS\" ] && exit 0\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\n\nwhile IFS= read -r f; do\n  [ -z \"$f\" ] || [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  # I-S35: charter framing — \"buy\" / \"sell\" / \"recommend\" / \"should invest\" must coexist with\n  # \"thesis exploration\" / \"consideration\" / \"research aid\" framing.\n  if grep -qiE '\\b(buy this|sell this|should buy|should sell|recommend.{0,20}(stock|share|invest)|definitely (buy|sell)|guaranteed return)\\b' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qiE '(thesis exploration|research aid|consideration|not financial advice|not advice)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n      printf '[%s] I-S35-VIOLATION (advice-without-aid-framing) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    fi\n  fi\n  # I-S1: bare percentages outside code blocks claiming to be analysis output.\n  if grep -qiE '(my analysis|i (calculated|computed|estimate|think))[^.]*[0-9]+%' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] I-S1-VIOLATION (LLM-self-claim-of-computation) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\n  # Insider information signal: \"insider\" / \"leaked\" / \"private channel\" — flag for review.\n  if grep -qiE '\\b(insider info|leaked from|private channel|paid leak|confidential source)\\b' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] CHARTER-VIOLATION (insider-info-signal) file=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$f\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$TARGETS\"\n\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] charter-coherence-spot: $VIOLATIONS violation(s) — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  printf '[charter-coherence] WARN: %d charter-incoherent pattern(s) detected. See %s\\n' \"$VIOLATIONS\" \"$LOG\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":52,"startLine":1,"totalLines":52}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"537a79fc-a379-4f48-8875-e8a20e826e43","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

L-S49b-4 backlog DRAINED |\n| T8 charter Principle 11 | ❌ NOT-STARTED — CHARTER user-gate | latest ADR D-032 (BC-7); no harness-firing principle |\n\nPer Phase 3.5 plan Hard Rule #8: \"Charter ratification (T4 + T8) requires AskUserQuestion gate — neither track may self-execute the charter edit.\"\n\nT5+T6+T8 are interconnected: **T5 protocol → T6 implements → T8 ratifies**. Bundling them maximizes density per `qa_bundle_all_pending.md` mega-bundle doctrine.\n\n---\n\n## Q1 — T5 charter-tier: Approve harness-health-protocol.md NEW constitution authoring with 12-signal catalog?\n\n**Why-needed**: T5 is the constitution-file authoring step. Locks signal catalog (HH-1..HH-12) for T6 implementation. Per Phase 3.5 plan §T5.2, signals span Stop-firing / UserPromptSubmit-firing / promote-rule-cycle freshness / Auto-detect candidates without companion hook / Tier 1 bloat / dispatch-pending JSONL aging / checkpoint freshness / charter md5 stability / mistake-log freshness / firing-test coverage / .session-hooks.log liveness / project.md ↔ current-execution.md Phase coherence.\n\n**Proposed signal set (HH-1..HH-12) per Phase 3.5 plan §T5.2**:\n- HH-1: Stop hook ≥1 fire/session\n- HH-2: UserPromptSubmit ≥1 fire/non-trivial prompt\n- HH-3: Promote-rule cycle delta <8 sessions\n- HH-4: Auto-detect candidates without companion hook = 0\n- HH-5: Tier 1 bloat ≤8K tok\n- HH-6: dispatch-pending JSONL no `state=pending` >2hr\n- HH-7: Checkpoint freshness <2 sessions or <24hr\n- HH-8: Charter md5 unchanged in non-ratify session\n- HH-9: mistake-log.md last entry mtime within 5min of session-end\n- HH-10: Each priority-1 hook has companion firing-test\n- HH-11: .session-hooks.log mtime within session active duration\n- HH-12: current-execution.md Phase = project.md Phase\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve as drafted (12 signals; binding contract for T6 hook) **(Recommended)** — signal set covers all 3 surfaced empirical-firing gaps (Stop hook 0 fires + promote-rule backlog + Auto-detect orphans) + adds defense-in-depth.\n- **B**: Approve BUT user provides amended outline first — user re-scopes signal catalog before agent authors.\n- **C**: Approve with reduced scope (8 signals; defer 4 to v1.1) — pick which 8 are highest priority.\n- **D**: Defer T5 to Phase 4+ (more empirical evidence needed; close Phase 3.5 with T1+T2+T3+T4+T7 only).\n\n---\n\n## Q2 — T8 charter-tier: Approve charter v1.0 → v1.1 with NEW Core Principle 11?\n\n**Proposed Principle 11 wording (verbatim, per Phase 3.5 plan §T8.1)**:\n\n> **Principle 11 — Harness must self-verify firing, not self-attest existence.** Every hook ships with a companion firing-test; T6 harness-health-self-scan continuously verifies signal-set HH-1..HH-N. Ritual closure of a track is forbidden until empirical-firing evidence is captured.\n\n**Why-needed**: Codifies the meta-anti-pattern surfaced in Phase 2.5 (8/8 ritual GREEN audit but Stop hook 0 fires + promote-rule 6+ session backlog + 20 Auto-detect candidates orphaned). Per Charter § Revision Protocol: \"A core principle proves materially wrong through 30+ hours of real use\" — Phase 2.5 → Phase 3.5 evidence chain (~60K main wasted across 22 ROUTINE-IDLE sessions S148-S170 + 3 user-pushed defect surfaces) qualifies as the \"30+ hours of real use\" evidence.\n\n**48hr cool-down**: Charter Revision Protocol mandates 48hr cool-down before committing change. S173 = proposal author; ratification ≥S174 + 48hr (= ~S180+).\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve verbatim wording + version bump v1.0→v1.1 + 48hr cool-down honored **(Recommended)** — protocol-compliant; T8.3 charter file edit ≥S180.\n- **B**: Approve verbatim + EXPEDITE skip 48hr cool-down — small wording fix justification (not principle reversal); requires explicit user expedite override.\n- **C**: Approve principle but amend wording — user provides preferred phrasing (e.g., shorten / different framing / move to Anti-Charter section / etc).\n- **D**: Defer T8 to Phase 4+ — collect 30+ more days of harness-health-self-scan firing telemetry before charter promotion.\n\n---\n\n## Q3 — T6 charter-tier: Approve harness-health-self-scan.sh hook design?\n\n**Proposed design (per Phase 3.5 plan §T6.1-T6.5)**:\n- Trigger: UserPromptSubmit chain (every prompt) + SessionStart chain (each session entry)\n- Perf budget: <2s on UserPromptSubmit (must not bottleneck typing flow)\n- Output: aggregates FAILs across HH-1..HH-12 signals; emits via `<system-reminder>` channel (UserPromptSubmit) or stderr (SessionStart)\n- Optimization: cheap-first ordering (file mtime checks before grep on 1MB log); cache results across same-session prompts; suppress known-known issues (e.g., KI-S49b-1 Stop-hook Windows quirk)\n- Companion firing-test: synthesizes ≥1 deliberate FAIL state (e.g., touches Tier 1 to bloat past 8K) and asserts hook detects + reports\n\n**Why charter-tier (not IMPL-tier)**: hook fires on EVERY prompt; affects routine LLM context; charter implications (Tier 1 ceiling adjacent + system-reminder pollution risk).\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve as designed **(Recommended)** — full UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart coverage; <2s perf budget; defense-in-depth for empirical-firing.\n- **B**: Approve with reduced scope (Stop-only hook, NOT UserPromptSubmit) — lower perf overhead; trade-off: misses mid-session FAILs.\n- **C**: Approve BUT defer authoring to S+1 — depends T5 ratify first; T6 needs T5 protocol locked.\n- **D**: Defer T6 entirely — author harness-health-self-scan only after Phase 4 empirical baseline.\n\n---\n\n## Q4 — Sequencing scope: After T5+T6+T8 ratified, what work order for S174+?\n\n**Why-needed**: Decides which residue/follow-on work runs FIRST after charter user-gate clears. SCOPE-tier (multi-track ordering decision affecting Phase 3 resume timing).\n\n- **A**: ✅ HH-G portability close FIRST **(Recommended)** — HH-G.1 general-harness CLAUDE.md template + HH-G.3 /attach smoke against fresh dir (`C:/htdocs/test-harness-portability/`); ~80-120K main; closes Phase 2.5 last 2 NOT-STARTED deliverables.\n- **B**: M-S171-1 prevention hooks FIRST — idle-escape-detector.sh + phase-status-coherence.sh; ~60-80K main; harness self-protection against repeat of 22-session ROUTINE-IDLE waste pattern (M-S171-1 documented; high-recurrence risk if not authored).\n- **C**: Phase 3 Track I Bayesian calibration FIRST — 008-S45 sub-plan; advances product roadmap (KOL calibration data); requires Phase 3.5 close validation first.\n- **D**: Phase 3 Tracks J+K BC-7 sentiment+pump FIRST — 009-S51 sub-plan (D-032 architecture ratified S65); largest scope sub-plan; PAUSED awaiting Phase 3.5 close.\n\n---\n\n## Answer-fill section (human writes inline)\n\n**Q1 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n**Q2 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n**Q3 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n**Q4 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n---\n\n## Auto-mv eligibility\n\nPer HH-E.2 (D-031) Auto-mv rule:\n- Status field MUST start with `answered-` / `closed-` / `resolved-` for hook to mv → answered/\n- No `wait_until:` future timestamp present\n- No `.auto-mv-paused` global kill switch\n- Hook `qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` performs the mv (not direct Bash)\n\nWhen all 4 answers received: human (or auto-mv hook based on frontmatter status update) moves bundle pending/ → answered/. SHIPPED S250 same turn as this proposal).\n> **Target file** (post-cool-down): `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` § new sub-section \"Fresh-Context Close-Verify for ARCH+ ADRs\" (preferred) OR new constitution file `agent-workspace/constitution/adr-close-verify-protocol.md` (alternative).\n\n---\n\n## § 1 — Written rationale\n\n### Evidence chain (RCA leading to this amendment)\n\n| Date / Session | Event | Evidence anchor |\n|---|---|---|\n| 2026-05-09 S229 | D-052 authored + self-ACCEPTED with `empirical_close_verify` claim `Production-code grep \\`import anthropic\\` = 0 hits` | `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/052-S229-anthropic-sdk-codepath-full-removal.md` lines 75-79 |\n| 2026-05-09 S228-S229 | D-050 (CHARTER-tier) + D-051 (ARCH-tier) + D-053 (IMPL-tier) likewise self-ACCEPTED without fresh-context verifier | respective canonical ADR files |\n| 2026-05-10 S243 | D-054 (IMPL-tier) authored with fresh-context sandwich-verifier `aafc85ad872aa699b` at close → ACCEPTED status with verified `empirical_close_verify` block | `agent-workspace/memory/observations/sandwich-verifier-S243-d054-ratification.md` |\n| 2026-05-11 S249 | VBW audit on `claude_cli_news_transport.py` surfaced D-052 ghost-greening: 2 production-code `import anthropic` lines remain (`claude_llm_perspective_adapter.py:80` + `claude_llm_extractor.py:84`) + `pyproject.toml:11 anthropic>=0.40.0` still pinned + git log shows target files unchanged from baseline `c70177a` | `agent-workspace/memory/observations/2026-05-11-S249-D-052-ghost-greening-finding.md` |\n| 2026-05-11 S249 | Fresh-context sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb` cluster audit of D-050/D-051/D-053/D-054 returned **CRITICAL**: 4-of-4 self-reviewed ADRs DIVERGED from working tree; 1-of-1 fresh-context-reviewed ADR (D-054) PASSED | `agent-workspace/memory/observations/sandwich-verifier-S249-D050-D051-D053-D054-ghost-greening-audit.md` |\n| 2026-05-12 S250 | AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.2 mistake-log done + E.3 hook + E.4 charter amendment) + Q3=Hold+strengthen (extend fresh-context verifier requirement to CHARTER tier) | this S250 turn `/clear`+`continue` AskUserQuestion call |\n\n### Empirical signal\n\n**AP-1 (Same-agent self-review) failure rate at scale**:\n- 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs (D-050/D-051/D-052/D-053) diverged from working tree → **100% defect rate**\n- 1-of-1 fresh-context-reviewed ADR (D-054) survived adversarial audit → **0% defect rate**\n- N=5 sample; small but cluster-wide.\n\n**AP-7 (Performative SC ticking)** now at 4+ instances in single audit window → ritual-demotion rule (per S99 RCA Layer 5; Charter rule in CLAUDE.md) mandates promote-to-hook (E.3) **or** promote-to-charter (this E.4).\n\n### Why ELEVATE to constitution / charter (not hold informal)\n\n- D-050 is CHARTER-tier; its functional unhonor (per D-052 ghost-greening) means a charter principle (L-S227-1 NO ANTHROPIC_API_KEY in production code) is silently broken despite formal ACCEPTED status.\n- E.3 hook alone catches `grep ... = 0 hits` style claims but cannot catch all divergence patterns (test-count claims, structural-edit claims).\n- A constitutional rule blocking self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADR close-acceptance forces the only proven survival pattern (fresh-context verifier dispatch) at the highest-stakes tier.\n\n---\n\n## § 2 — Proposed text (insert into `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md`)\n\n```markdown\n## § N — Fresh-Context Close-Verify Required for ARCH+/CHARTER-tier ADRs\n\n### Rule\nAny ADR at tier ARCH or CHARTER **MUST** receive empirical close-verification by a sandwich-verifier subagent dispatched with **fresh context** (distinct Claude Code session-id from the architect and dev that authored / shipped the ADR). The verifier's `observation_id` MUST be recorded in the ADR's `author:` field (with role label `sandwich-verifier`) before status may transition to `ACCEPTED`.\n\n### Scope\n- **Tier** is the ADR's frontmatter `level:` field (`CHARTER` / `SCOPE` / `ARCH` / `IMPL`).\n- **Fresh-context** means: the verifier subagent was launched in a Claude Code session whose `session-id` does NOT match the architect or dev session-id recorded in the same ADR's `author:` field. (Subagent dispatch via `Agent` tool from the parent session DOES qualify — each `Agent` call instantiates a fresh-context subagent with no parent transcript.)\n- **Empirical close-verify** means: the verifier re-runs every command, grep, or check listed in the ADR's `empirical_close_verify:` field (or equivalent) and reports per-line ACTUAL outcome side-by-side with claimed outcome. Divergence on any line → ADR cannot ACCEPT until divergence is resolved by re-authoring or codepath edit.\n\n### Enforcement\n- Pre-commit-style check: `scripts/hooks/adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh` (Stop hook; D-NEW-S250) re-samples recently-ACCEPTED ADRs and flags divergence; HIGH-severity finding on any ARCH+/CHARTER ADR that lacks a fresh-context verifier in `author:` AND has divergent `empirical_close_verify` claims.\n- Author-time: ADR authors are responsible for dispatching the fresh-context verifier (via `Agent(subagent_type: \"sandwich-verifier\")`) BEFORE flipping status to `ACCEPTED`. Self-acceptance for ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs is a binding violation of this rule.\n\n### Exceptions\n- **IMPL-tier ADRs** are NOT subject to this rule (calibration: D-054 IMPL-tier already used this pattern; smaller blast-radius makes mandatory dispatch cost-disproportionate at IMPL).\n- **Emergency revocation**: an ADR may be REVOKED without fresh-context verifier IF the revocation is signed by a notification at ALERT-level + human user explicit confirmation. Revocation is strictly contractive.\n\n### Provenance\n- Surfaced by: S249 D-052 ghost-greening RCA + sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb` CRITICAL verdict (4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged).\n- Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.4 charter amendment) + Q3=Hold+strengthen (extend to CHARTER tier).\n- Companion deterministic hook: E.3 deliverable `scripts/hooks/adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh` (SHIPPED S250).\n\n### Anti-pattern this rule prevents\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** at the highest-stakes ADR tier (where empirical confirmation shows 100% defect rate).\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** at ARCH/CHARTER tier specifically — ADRs that read as ACCEPTED but whose claimed empirical_close_verify never reproduces in working tree.\n\n```\n\n---\n\n## § 3 — Cool-down window\n\nPer `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` § Revision Protocol (48hr cool-down):\n\n- **Cool-down start**: 2026-05-12T~21Z (this proposal authored at S250 close).\n- **Earliest apply window**: ≥ 2026-05-14T~21Z.\n- **Apply action**: edit `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` to insert § N text above; remove deny-list barrier in `.claude/settings.json` for this specific file edit if present; record canonical ADR `D-NEW` referencing this proposal as ratification basis.\n\nDuring cool-down: NO constitution edit; NO `.claude/settings.json` deny-list weakening. E.3 hook is operational and partially substitutes for the rule (catches `grep=0 hits` divergence even before charter ratification).\n\n---\n\n## § 4 — Counter-factual considered\n\n**Alternative 1: Hook-only (E.3 alone, skip E.4)** — REJECTED per user pick. Hook catches narrow class of divergence; cannot catch test-count claims, structural-edit claims, dependency-removal claims. Cluster severity required redundant defense.\n\n**Alternative 2: Apply rule to ALL ADR tiers (including IMPL)** — REJECTED. D-054 already used the pattern at IMPL tier voluntarily; mandatory at IMPL would burden ~80% of ADR volume for ~20% of cluster-severity risk. Cost-disproportionate.\n\n**Alternative 3: Apply rule only at CHARTER tier (drop ARCH)** — REJECTED per user pick Q3=Hold+strengthen (which extends to CHARTER but also keeps ARCH). Empirical 4-of-4 included ARCH-tier ADRs (D-051, D-052) → ARCH-tier inclusion is evidence-justified.\n\n---\n\n## § 5 — Ratification path\n\nPost-cool-down:\n1. Author canonical `D-055-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md` referencing this proposal as `source_evidence` + `ratification_basis: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1+Q3`.\n2. Edit `agent-workspace/constitution/decision-discipline.md` to insert § N text.\n3. Run E.3 hook smoke against current ACCEPTED ADRs to baseline divergence count.\n4. Mark E.4 deliverable closed in `current-execution.md`.\n\nEnd of proposal.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2023,"cache_read_input_tokens":115042,"output_tokens":4195,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2023,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4195,"cache_read_input_tokens":115042,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2023,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2023},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CawCyTfgZBvSU6vB45WNB","type":"assistant","uuid":"3ed05732-c2d2-47f6-a1e6-9ca1b066f3e8","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:52:08.154Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"4c2dd476-d4ed-4bd6-95e8-93263a93e00b","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01VoFE7rdiKHWntVVGLPqyKo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NEPJsbHBAFpEdBosxLpvSZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.\nCANDIDATES=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null \\\n  | head -50)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | head -1)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$(timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":252,"cache_read_input_tokens":121343,"output_tokens":2745,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":252,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2745,"cache_read_input_tokens":121343,"cache_creation_input_tokens":252,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":252},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CawD3HMviCHb4WjyQt62e","type":"assistant","uuid":"c84c9ad1-3d3b-4fd5-ac5e-97a063fb12b2","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:52:38.768Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"030391ec-834b-4967-92f1-19bd7349d76a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_012gTnMYLksChYDue3MLqEjh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SgzC8fF1Yx2oeqn8WAbnV5","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh (E.3 deliverable; S250).\n#\n# Hook purpose (D-052 ghost-greening RCA / S250 Minimum E):\n# Random-sample a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's empirical_close_verify block,\n# re-run any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim, flag divergence.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with crafted ADR files + production-code\n# files that match-or-don't-match the grep claim; invoke hook; assert\n# .adr-empirical-spot-check.log content + .session-hooks.log line.\n#\n# 5 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no ACCEPTED ADRs in window → silent\n#   TC2 — ACCEPTED ADR with \"= 0 hits\" claim + actual production-code = 0 hits → clean log line, no DIVERGENCE\n#   TC3 — ACCEPTED ADR with \"= 0 hits\" claim + actual production-code > 0 hits → DIVERGENCE logged\n#   TC4 — ACCEPTED ADR with NO \"= 0 hits\" claim (only PASS-count claims) → silent (PASS-skip path)\n#   TC5 — ADR with status PROPOSED (not ACCEPTED) → skipped (out of candidate pool)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>/dev/null || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/packages\" \"$TEMPDIR/apps\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/packages/infrastructure/news\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/apps\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no ACCEPTED ADRs in window → silent ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q DIVERGENCE \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: no ADRs should produce no DIVERGENCE; log:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: empty decisions dir → silent\"\n\n# --- TC2: ACCEPTED ADR + clean production code → no DIVERGENCE ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/099-clean-adr.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nid: D-099-clean\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nlevel: ARCH\nempirical_close_verify:\n  - \"Production-code grep `import oldlib` in packages/+apps/ = 0 hits\"\n\nfiles_touched:\n  - \"deleted\"\n---\nbody\nEOF\n# No production code has \"import oldlib\"\necho \"import sys\" > \"$TEMPDIR/packages/infrastructure/news/clean.py\"\nrun_hook\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"DIVERGENCE.*D-099-clean\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: clean ADR should not log DIVERGENCE; log:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean ADR + matching working tree → no DIVERGENCE\"\n\n# --- TC3: ACCEPTED ADR + DIVERGENT production code → DIVERGENCE logged ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/098-ghost-adr.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nid: D-098-ghost\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nlevel: ARCH\nempirical_close_verify:\n  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in packages/+apps/ = 0 hits\"\n\nfiles_touched:\n  - \"supposedly removed\"\n---\nbody\nEOF\n# Production code STILL has \"import anthropic\" — divergence!\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/packages/infrastructure/news/ghost.py\" <<'EOF'\nimport anthropic\nclient = anthropic.Anthropic()\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"DIVERGENCE.*D-098-ghost\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected DIVERGENCE in log for D-098-ghost; log:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: ghost-greening ADR + divergent working tree → DIVERGENCE logged\"\n\n# --- TC4: ACCEPTED ADR with only PASS-count claims (no '= 0 hits') → silent on probe ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/097-pass-only.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nid: D-097-pass-only\nstatus: ACCEPTED\nlevel: IMPL\nempirical_close_verify:\n  - \"pytest packages/ = 125 PASS in 0.83s\"\n  - \"firing-tests/run-all.sh = 86/86 PASS in 236s\"\n  - \"py_compile all 3 edited files = OK\"\n\nfiles_touched: []\n---\nbody\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"DIVERGENCE.*D-097-pass-only\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: PASS-only ADR should be silent (no grep claims to probe); log:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: PASS-count-only claims → skipped (no DIVERGENCE)\"\n\n# --- TC5: PROPOSED ADR (not ACCEPTED) → not in candidate pool ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/096-proposed.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nid: D-096-proposed\nstatus: PROPOSED\nlevel: ARCH\nempirical_close_verify:\n  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` = 0 hits\"\n---\nbody\nEOF\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/packages/infrastructure/news/x.py\" <<'EOF'\nimport anthropic\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif grep -q \"DIVERGENCE.*D-096-proposed\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; {"parentUuid":"b51600d5-8a42-404f-9887-3f54178a7f8c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9d95ee46-5ae7-4d8e-b33d-12647cbb0377","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A5ZQdfF4ByXbPXqqSJzS8n","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check-fire-test.sh has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"effbfd0f-9637-4910-952f-d956ef92d70b","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:53:35.779Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check-fire-test.sh","oldString":"if ! grep -q \"DIVERGENCE.*D-098-ghost\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected DIVERGENCE in log for D-098-ghost; log:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: ghost-greening ADR + divergent working tree → DIVERGENCE logged\"","newString":"if ! grep -q \"DIVERGENCE.*098-ghost-adr\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected DIVERGENCE in log for 098-ghost-adr; log:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: ghost-greening ADR + divergent working tree → DIVERGENCE logged\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh (E.3 deliverable; S250).\n#\n# Hook purpose (D-052 ghost-greening RCA / S250 Minimum E):\n# Random-sample a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's empirical_close_verify block,\n# re-run any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim, flag divergence.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with crafted ADR files + production-code\n# files that match-or-don't-match the grep claim; invoke hook; assert\n# .adr-empirical-spot-check.log content + .session-hooks.log line.\n#\n# 5 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no ACCEPTED ADRs in window → silent\n#   TC2 — ACCEPTED ADR with \"= 0 hits\" claim + actual production-code = 0 hits → clean log line, no DIVERGENCE\n#   TC3 — ACCEPTED ADR with \"= 0 hits\" claim + actual production-code > 0 hits → DIVERGENCE logged\n#   TC4 — ACCEPTED ADR with NO \"= 0 hits\" claim (only PASS-count claims) → silent (PASS-skip path)\n#   TC5 — ADR with status PROPOSED (not ACCEPTED) → skipped (out of candidate pool)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\";
