} | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.\nCANDIDATES=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null \\\n  | head -50)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | head -1)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$(timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":73,"oldLines":10,"newStart":73,"newLines":10,"lines":["   ACTUAL_HITS=0","   for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do","     [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue","-    H=\"$(timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\","+    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\","         --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\","         --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\","-        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\"","+        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"","     ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))","   done"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7a1859d5-6763-4b4b-ab3b-e84a78909f85","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

-f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.\nCANDIDATES=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null \\\n  | head -50)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | head -1)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":30,"oldLines":14,"newStart":30,"newLines":14,"lines":[" [ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0"," "," # Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.","-CANDIDATES=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\","-  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null \\","-  | head -50)\"","+CANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\","+  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\","+  | head -50 || true)\""," "," [ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0"," "," # Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.","-PICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | head -1)\"","+PICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | { shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || head -1; } || true)\""," [ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0"," "," # Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key)."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ec6e3e80-370c-4ef4-9dfe-39f21af6cfec","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

-d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done","new_string":"  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.\n  GREP_FLAG=P\n  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    GREP_FLAG=E\n  fi\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1317,"cache_read_input_tokens":143568,"output_tokens":848,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1317,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":848,"cache_read_input_tokens":143568,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1317,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1317},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CawDJEeNzsaFrZez3piJY","type":"assistant","uuid":"4c906d0a-c36e-4061-9d5c-fdbb27034b91","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:55:44.476Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"4c906d0a-c36e-4061-9d5c-fdbb27034b91","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9d95ee46-5ae7-4d8e-b33d-12647cbb0377","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WrHX9VumshdnnjgjX6oV6N","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"daa93664-8d6a-4a98-89c2-72675f79657f","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:55:45.020Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh","oldString":"  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done","newString":"  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.\n  GREP_FLAG=P\n  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    GREP_FLAG=E\n  fi\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.\nCANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\\n  | head -50 || true)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | { shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || head -1; } || true)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":70,"oldLines":10,"newStart":70,"newLines":15,"lines":["   esac"," ","   # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).","+  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.","+  GREP_FLAG=P","+  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then","+    GREP_FLAG=E","+  fi","   ACTUAL_HITS=0","   for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do","     [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue","-    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rE \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\","+    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\","         --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\","         --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\","         --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4c906d0a-c36e-4061-9d5c-fdbb27034b91","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"802efbec-c49e-4bcf-9fb1-ba93cb66451f","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:56:29.956Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh","oldString":"# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.\nCANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\\n  | head -50 || true)\"","newString":"# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs (any age) that have an empirical_close_verify field.\n# Why no -mtime filter: only a minority of ADRs use empirical_close_verify, and only those\n# are worth probing. Filtering by field membership is the actual cost-bounder.\nCANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^empirical_close_verify:' {} 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\\n  | head -50 || true)\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.\nCANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\\n  | head -50 || true)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | { shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || head -1; } || true)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.\n  GREP_FLAG=P\n  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    GREP_FLAG=E\n  fi\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":29,"oldLines":8,"newStart":29,"newLines":11,"lines":[" "," [ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0"," ","-# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs touched in last 30 days, excluding README/templates.","-CANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' -mtime -30 2>/dev/null \\","+# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs (any age) that have an empirical_close_verify field.","+# Why no -mtime filter: only a minority of ADRs use empirical_close_verify, and only those","+# are worth probing. Filtering by field membership is the actual cost-bounder.","+CANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' 2>/dev/null \\","+  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^empirical_close_verify:' {} 2>/dev/null \\","   | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\","   | head -50 || true)\""," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9c7158c2-16e8-4ab7-b197-55b3798d7b0f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"9cb6fc01-9b63-465c-a59f-10a1d8998fa1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01WASXHQqPKY23wZ7UmT2Czy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Fr87WpwhzCyBRbuWPtP1L5","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh","old_string":"  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.\n  GREP_FLAG=P\n  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    GREP_FLAG=E\n  fi\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done","new_string":"  # Probe strategy: ADR claim patterns often contain YAML-escaped regex (e.g. `\\\\s`, `\\\\b`,\n  # `(?:...)`) that don't survive shell var expansion cleanly. Use longest-literal-token\n  # heuristic: extract the longest unbroken alphabetic substring (>=5 chars) and probe for\n  # it as a fixed string. Lossy but reliably catches gross AP-7 ghost-greening cases\n  # (e.g. \"anthropic\" claimed 0-hits but actually present).\n  PROBE_TOKEN=\"$(echo \"$PATTERN\" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_]{5,}' | awk '{ if (length($0) > max) { max=length($0); tok=$0 } } END { print tok }')\"\n  [ -z \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" ] && continue\n\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rF \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":877,"cache_read_input_tokens":150453,"output_tokens":1977,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":877,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1977,"cache_read_input_tokens":150453,"cache_creation_input_tokens":877,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":877},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CawDQ6hKKGRgkon2SXYfs","type":"assistant","uuid":"5dd32021-e6c2-4570-b5ef-77393b4dfaca","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:57:15.180Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"3c38d019-42b9-425e-b05e-5d5665d58bdd","timestamp":"2026-05-11T17:57:15.735Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh","oldString":"  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.\n  GREP_FLAG=P\n  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    GREP_FLAG=E\n  fi\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done","newString":"  # Probe strategy: ADR claim patterns often contain YAML-escaped regex (e.g. `\\\\s`, `\\\\b`,\n  # `(?:...)`) that don't survive shell var expansion cleanly. Use longest-literal-token\n  # heuristic: extract the longest unbroken alphabetic substring (>=5 chars) and probe for\n  # it as a fixed string. Lossy but reliably catches gross AP-7 ghost-greening cases\n  # (e.g. \"anthropic\" claimed 0-hits but actually present).\n  PROBE_TOKEN=\"$(echo \"$PATTERN\" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_]{5,}' | awk '{ if (length($0) > max) { max=length($0); tok=$0 } } END { print tok }')\"\n  [ -z \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" ] && continue\n\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rF \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs (any age) that have an empirical_close_verify field.\n# Why no -mtime filter: only a minority of ADRs use empirical_close_verify, and only those\n# are worth probing. Filtering by field membership is the actual cost-bounder.\nCANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^empirical_close_verify:' {} 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\\n  | head -50 || true)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | { shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || head -1; } || true)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).\n  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.\n  GREP_FLAG=P\n  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    GREP_FLAG=E\n  fi\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":72,"oldLines":16,"newStart":72,"newLines":18,"lines":["     *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;","   esac"," ","-  # Re-run grep with bounded timeout; count hits across scan targets (excluding tests).","-  # Prefer -P (Perl regex; matches ADR claim syntax `(?:...)`, `\\s`, `\\b`); fall back to -E.","-  GREP_FLAG=P","-  if ! echo \"\" | grep -P \"\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then","-    GREP_FLAG=E","-  fi","+  # Probe strategy: ADR claim patterns often contain YAML-escaped regex (e.g. `\\\\s`, `\\\\b`,","+  # `(?:...)`) that don't survive shell var expansion cleanly. Use longest-literal-token","+  # heuristic: extract the longest unbroken alphabetic substring (>=5 chars) and probe for","+  # it as a fixed string. Lossy but reliably catches gross AP-7 ghost-greening cases","+  # (e.g. \"anthropic\" claimed 0-hits but actually present).","+  PROBE_TOKEN=\"$(echo \"$PATTERN\" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_]{5,}' | awk '{ if (length($0) > max) { max=length($0); tok=$0 } } END { print tok }')\"","+  [ -z \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" ] && continue","+","   ACTUAL_HITS=0","   for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do","     [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue","-    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -r\"$GREP_FLAG\" \"$PATTERN\" \"$target\" \\","+    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rF \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" \"$target\" \\","         --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\","         --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\","         --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5dd32021-e6c2-4570-b5ef-77393b4dfaca","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" probe_token=%s actual_hits=%d\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh — random-samples a recently-ACCEPTED ADR's\n# empirical_close_verify field, re-runs any \"grep ... = 0 hits\" claim against the\n# working tree, and flags divergence.\n#\n# Surfaced by: D-052 ghost-greening RCA (S249 sandwich-verifier `a0522171e2f84c5bb`\n#   CRITICAL — 4-of-4 self-reviewed ARCH+/CHARTER ADRs diverged from working tree).\n# Ratified by: S250 AskUserQuestion Q1=Minimum E (E.3 hook authorization).\n# Companion artifact: agent-workspace/proposals/E4-fresh-context-verifier-arch-charter-tier.md\n#\n# Hook behavior:\n# - Pick ONE random ACCEPTED ADR mtime <= 30 days from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/\n# - Parse its empirical_close_verify: block for lines matching ` = 0 hits` (and variants)\n# - Extract the grep PATTERN between backticks on the same line\n# - Re-run grep against packages/ + apps/\n# - If actual hit count > 0 → emit WARN to log + system-reminder for agent visibility\n#\n# Operates as Stop hook; non-blocking (set -o pipefail traps to exit 0).\n# Bounded budget: max 5s execution (timeout wrapper on grep).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.adr-empirical-spot-check.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Candidate pool: ACCEPTED ADRs (any age) that have an empirical_close_verify field.\n# Why no -mtime filter: only a minority of ADRs use empirical_close_verify, and only those\n# are worth probing. Filtering by field membership is the actual cost-bounder.\nCANDIDATES=\"$( { find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^empirical_close_verify:' {} 2>/dev/null \\\n  | xargs -I{} grep -l '^status:[[:space:]]*ACCEPTED' {} 2>/dev/null || true; } \\\n  | head -50 || true)\"\n\n[ -z \"$CANDIDATES\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Random pick (deterministic-ish via $RANDOM); 1 sample per fire keeps cost bounded.\nPICK=\"$(echo \"$CANDIDATES\" | { shuf -n 1 2>/dev/null || head -1; } || true)\"\n[ -z \"$PICK\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PICK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Extract empirical_close_verify block (YAML-list under top-level key).\n# Lines look like:  - \"Production-code grep `import anthropic` in apps/+packages/ = 0 hits\"\nVERIFY_BLOCK=\"$(awk '\n  /^empirical_close_verify:/ {capture=1; next}\n  capture && /^[a-z_]+:/ && !/^[[:space:]]*-/ {capture=0}\n  capture {print}\n' \"$PICK\")\"\n\n[ -z \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Look for \"= 0 hits\" style claims.\nZERO_CLAIMS=\"$(echo \"$VERIFY_BLOCK\" | grep -E '=[[:space:]]*0[[:space:]]+(hits?|results?|matches?)' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" ] && exit 0\n\nADR_ID=\"$(basename \"$PICK\" .md)\"\nDIVERGENCE_COUNT=0\nSCAN_TARGETS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/packages $PROJECT_DIR/apps\"\n\nwhile IFS= read -r claim_line; do\n  [ -z \"$claim_line\" ] && continue\n  # Extract pattern between first pair of backticks on the claim line.\n  PATTERN=\"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | sed -n 's/.*`\\([^`]*\\)`.*/\\1/p' | head -1)\"\n  [ -z \"$PATTERN\" ] && continue\n\n  # Skip patterns that look like pytest output rather than greppable code patterns.\n  case \"$PATTERN\" in\n    *PASS*|*pytest*|*py_compile*) continue ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Probe strategy: ADR claim patterns often contain YAML-escaped regex (e.g. `\\\\s`, `\\\\b`,\n  # `(?:...)`) that don't survive shell var expansion cleanly. Use longest-literal-token\n  # heuristic: extract the longest unbroken alphabetic substring (>=5 chars) and probe for\n  # it as a fixed string. Lossy but reliably catches gross AP-7 ghost-greening cases\n  # (e.g. \"anthropic\" claimed 0-hits but actually present).\n  PROBE_TOKEN=\"$(echo \"$PATTERN\" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_]{5,}' | awk '{ if (length($0) > max) { max=length($0); tok=$0 } } END { print tok }')\"\n  [ -z \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" ] && continue\n\n  ACTUAL_HITS=0\n  for target in $SCAN_TARGETS; do\n    [ ! -d \"$target\" ] && continue\n    H=\"$( { timeout 5 grep -rF \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" \"$target\" \\\n        --include='*.py' --include='*.toml' \\\n        --exclude-dir='__pycache__' --exclude-dir='.pytest_cache' \\\n        --exclude='test_*.py' --exclude='*_test.py' 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l)\"\n    ACTUAL_HITS=$(( ACTUAL_HITS + H ))\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))\n    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\\n      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\"\n\nif [ \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  # Emit system-reminder (stdout JSON for Stop hook surface; falls back to stderr WARN).\n  cat <<JSON 2>/dev/null || printf '[adr-empirical-spot-check] WARN: %d divergence(s) in %s; see %s\\n' \"$DIVERGENCE_COUNT\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$LOG\" >&2\n{\"hookEventName\":\"Stop\",\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"additionalContext\":\"adr-empirical-spot-check: $DIVERGENCE_COUNT divergence(s) in $ADR_ID — empirical_close_verify claims diverge from working tree. See $LOG for details. Consider fresh-context sandwich-verifier dispatch (E.4 charter rule, post-cool-down).\"}}\nJSON\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)] adr-empirical-spot-check: ${ADR_ID} clean (${ZERO_CLAIMS:+probed }$(echo \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\" | wc -l) claim(s))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":92,"oldLines":8,"newStart":92,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ","   if [ \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then","     DIVERGENCE_COUNT=$(( DIVERGENCE_COUNT + 1 ))","-    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" actual_hits=%d pattern=`%s`\\n' \\","-      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" \"$PATTERN\" >> \"$LOG\"","+    printf '[%s] DIVERGENCE adr=%s claim=\"%s\" probe_token=%s actual_hits=%d\\n' \\","+      \"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\" \"$ADR_ID\" \"$(echo \"$claim_line\" | tr -d '\\n' | head -c 200)\" \"$PROBE_TOKEN\" \"$ACTUAL_HITS\" >> \"$LOG\"","   fi"," done <<< \"$ZERO_CLAIMS\""," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9f69cac2-9c07-4206-99d7-529b4315c623","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"93671bf4-b7b2-450b-bd1e-31d68387caca","version":"2.1.137","gitBranch":"main"}

Adding T5 constitution doc + T8 charter principle is **documentation work**, not new behavior.\n- Phase 4 entry should NOT block on documentation backlog when the underlying behaviors are already operational and tested.\n- Cluster Minimum E follow-on (D-052 ghost-greening) is more time-critical than T5/T8 documentation; it touches CHARTER-tier rule L-S227-1 with empirical violation evidence.\n- T5 + T8 will be authored in dedicated post-Phase-4-entry sessions (likely Track 0 of Phase 4 master-plan housekeeping) so the documentation IS shipped — just on a different cadence.\n\n## Cluster Minimum E carryover (S250 follow-on)\n\nE.2 (mistake-log digest) DONE 2026-05-11 S249 (M-S249-2 CRITICAL appended).\nE.3 (`adr-empirical-close-verify-spot-check.sh` Stop hook) SHIPPED 2026-05-12 S250 — this turn.
